Method: ASPIRE包含三个组件：(1)统一混合前向，允许草稿与验证请求共存于同一批前向中，无需全局草稿-验证阶段；(2)轻量在线推测调度器，基于每请求接受率估计和批感知成本模型，让每个请求独立决定验证时机；(3)草稿内刷新层，在草稿阶段的指定层执行全注意力，每步更新稀疏上下文以减少陈旧性。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理与长上下文基准上，ASPIRE相比自回归基线解码吞吐提速1.70-4.58倍，相比最强先前的自推测基线平均提速约27%。

Conclusion: ASPIRE通过非同步批处理与动态推测调度，有效缓解长上下文解码的内存瓶颈，实现显著吞吐提升。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by attention, whose repeated KV-cache reads make decoding memory-bound. Self-speculative decoding alleviates this by drafting tokens with sparse attention and verifying them with full attention, but existing batched methods remain synchronized: all requests in a batch share a single draft-verify schedule, even though the optimal draft length varies widely across requests and changes dynamically within each request. We propose ASPIRE, a non-synchronized batched self-speculative decoding framework built on three components. First, a unified mixed forward allows drafting and verifying requests to coexist in the same batched forward pass, removing the need for global draft-verify phases. Second, a lightweight online speculation scheduler uses per-request acceptance-rate estimates and a batch-aware cost model to let each request independently choose when to verify. Third, an intra-draft refresh layer performs full attention at a single designated layer during drafting, updating the sparse context at every draft step to reduce staleness during drafting. Across three models and five reasoning and long-context benchmarks, ASPIRE achieves $1.70$-$4.58\times$ speedup in decoding throughput over autoregressive baselines and improves average speedup by approximately $27\%$ over the strongest prior self-speculative baselines.

</details>


### [213] [Maximum Strong Independent Sets in Hypergraphs: Reductions, Bounds, and Greedy Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17951)
*Yingquan,Wu,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限超图中的最大强独立集问题，即寻找与每条超边至多相交一个顶点的最大顶点集，并为此发展了一套关联结构分析工具，涵盖精确归约、上界推导、证书机制以及分层贪心聚类算法的理论与复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于:每个观测到的块可视为局部不相容约束，但跨重叠块进行传递闭包未必合理。典型动机是多频段 LSH-MinHash 去重，其中碰撞桶提供局部证据，而连通分量收缩会引入虚假的全局等价关系。

Method: 作者为最大强独立集问题开发了关联结构工具集：证明支配关系、关联孪生、权重为1的块的精确归约；推导闭式与低权重上界；引入刺穿证书与覆盖证书以强化这些上界；并分析一种由块权重和剩余关联驱动的分层贪心聚类算法，其分析包含可行性、极大性、条件最优性、分层见证匹配上界及关联局部复杂度界。

Result: 结果为广泛的关联族给出正确性、终止性、不动点与最优性证书，并通过示例展示不同证书在何种情况下相互分离或彼此重合。

Conclusion: 本文建立了最大强独立集问题的关联结构分析框架，结合精确归约、多种上界与证书技术及分层贪心算法，提供了系统的理论保证，并阐明了不同最优性证书之间的关系。

Abstract: We study the maximum strong independent set problem in a finite hypergraph: find the largest vertex set that intersects every hyperedge in at most one vertex. This objective arises whenever each observed block is a local incompatibility constraint but transitive closure across overlapping blocks is not justified. A motivating example is multi-band LSH-MinHash deduplication, where each collision bucket gives local evidence, while connected-component contraction can impose spurious global equivalences. The paper develops an incidence-structural toolkit for this problem. We prove exact reductions for dominance, incidence twins, and weight-1 blocks; derive closed-form and low-weight upper bounds; introduce puncturing and covering certificates that sharpen those bounds; and analyze a layered greedy clustering algorithm driven by block weights and residual incidence. The algorithmic analysis includes feasibility, maximality, conditional optimality, a layered witness-matching upper bound, and incidence-local complexity bounds. The results give correctness, termination, fixed-point, and optimality certificates for broad incidence families, together with examples showing when different certificates separate or coincide.

</details>


### [214] [QuanText: Protecting Dataset-Level Secrets in Textual Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17995)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuanText，一种免训练且与语言模型无关的文本数据发布机制，通过随机量化扰动秘密属性及相关属性分布，在保护数据集级全局秘密的同时保持数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据集被广泛用于下游应用与研究，但直接发布文本会泄露数据源的全局敏感属性（如性别、诊断或立场的比例）。现有研究多关注属性推断攻击，而针对数据集级秘密的防御手段有限；差分隐私虽能保护个体记录，但对聚合属性的保护较弱。

Method: 提出 QuanText，基于统计最大泄漏（SML）框架，构建覆盖秘密属性与非秘密属性的候选发布分布，随机选取一个与私有经验分布足够接近的候选分布，再利用原文本中与属性相关的片段重写每条私有文本样本，使其匹配所选分布。该方法免训练且不依赖特定大语言模型。

Result: 在理想条件下，证明 QuanText 满足 SML 保证；由于实际中这些条件未必严格成立，作者在真实数据集上进行实证评估，结果表明 QuanText 相比竞争性数据生成基线取得了更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: QuanText 为文本数据集提供了一种有效的全局秘密保护方案，在不牺牲数据效用的前提下优于现有基线，是数据集级隐私保护的有前景方向。

Abstract: Natural-language datasets support many downstream applications and research studies, but releasing text can reveal sensitive global properties of the underlying data source, such as the proportion of records associated with a particular gender, diagnosis, or political stance. Existing work has largely focused on property inference attacks that recover such global properties, while defenses for protecting these dataset-level secrets remain limited. Differential privacy, although effective for protecting individual records, provides only weak protection for aggregate properties. We propose Randomized Quantization for Text (QuanText), a training-free and large-language-model-agnostic data release mechanism that protects global secrets in textual datasets while preserving data utility. Given a dataset-level secret, such as the proportion of records with a particular diagnosis, and attributes whose utility should be preserved, such as topic and sentiment, QuanText perturbs both the secret distribution and the distributions of correlated attributes. It does so by constructing candidate release distributions over secret and non-secret attributes, randomly selecting a candidate sufficiently close to the private empirical distribution, and rewriting each private text sample to match the selected distribution using attribute-related snippets from the original text. QuanText is inspired by the Statistic Maximal Leakage (SML) framework, which bounds leakage about a secret function of a data distribution. Under idealized conditions, we show that QuanText satisfies an SML guarantee. Since these conditions may not hold exactly in practice, we also evaluate QuanText empirically on real-world datasets. Our results show that QuanText achieves a better empirical privacy-utility trade-off than competing data generation baselines.

</details>


### [215] [The Attention Within: Consensus Dynamics in Selective State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17997)
*João Pedro Silvestre,Álvaro Rodríguez Abella,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究选择性状态空间模型（SSM），将token跨层演化建模为常微分方程，证明其递归机制与Transformer注意力一样会驱动token趋于共识（consensus），并指出输出门是调控该共识程度的关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性SSM（如Mamba）已成为Transformer的有力替代方案，性能相当且推理效率更高。已有工作表明SSM递归可写成类似线性注意力的形式，两者架构比表面更接近。既然Transformer中的注意力会驱动token聚类、乃至坍缩到单一方向（共识），那么作者自然地追问：SSM核心的递归是否也会驱动token达成共识？

Method: 采用动力系统视角，将token跨层演化建模为常微分方程（ODE）。利用输入-状态稳定性（input-to-state stability）理论，针对时变权重矩阵的情形，证明共识平衡点的局部指数稳定性，并刻画其吸引域（domain of attraction）——这一时变设定是此前结果未处理的。

Result: 理论上证明了SSM递归同样会聚合token，与注意力行为一致，说明两者相似性比预期更深。数值实验在预训练的Mamba-2模型上进行，结果表明输出门（output gate）是调控共识程度的组件，它阻止token完全达成共识。

Conclusion: SSM与Transformer的相似性具有更深的动力学根源：二者核心机制都会驱动token趋于共识。输出门在此过程中起到调节作用，避免token完全坍缩，这为理解SSM的表达能力与设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a compelling alternative to transformers, combining competitive performance with substantially improved inference efficiency. At each SSM layer, a sequence of hidden states are propagated by a recurrence, mixing information of different tokens. Despite using a different mechanism, this mixing plays a role analogous to attention in transformers. In fact, recent works have shown that the two architectures may be closer than they first appear, as this recurrence admits a formulation akin to linear attention. In transformers, attention is known to drive the tokens to cluster, i.e., to reach consensus, collapsing in the limit to a single direction. Thus, we ask: does the recurrence at the core of SSMs drive the tokens to consensus, as attention does in transformers?
  To answer this question, we take a dynamical systems perspective on SSMs, modeling the evolution of tokens across layers as an ordinary differential equation. By exploiting input-to-state stability arguments, we establish local exponential stability of the consensus equilibria and characterize their domain of attraction for time-varying weight matrices, a setting not addressed by previous results. We thereby show that the resemblance between SSMs and transformers does run deeper: the recurrence at the core of SSMs aggregates tokens just as attention does. Numerical experiments on a pretrained Mamba-2 model point to the output gate as the component that regulates the extent of this consensus, preventing the tokens from reaching it in full.

</details>


### [216] [Structural Inference under Hidden Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18045)
*Zhongben Gong,Xiaoqun Wu,Mingyang Zhou,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIHA方法，解决"隐藏智能体下的结构推断"问题，通过结构无关初始化与结构引导迭代优化，联合恢复隐藏轨迹与交互结构，在三个基准动力系统和动作捕捉遮挡实验中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多智能体动力学中恢复潜在交互结构对理解与预测交互系统很重要。现有轨迹结构推断通常假设所有被建模智能体的轨迹均可获得，但实际部署中因传感受限、遮挡或通信失败，智能体可能无法被观测。已有工作涉及未见节点估计、部分观测下的结构推断和缺失值填补，但隐藏智能体轨迹与其交互的联合恢复仍研究不足，且存在循环依赖难题。

Method: 将问题形式化为"隐藏智能体下的结构推断"，并提出SIHA。该方法结合结构无关初始化与结构引导的迭代优化：从可见观测重建隐藏轨迹，利用神经关系推断（NRI）推断交互，再通过多强度结构注意力与迭代的状态—结构更新，将估计结构反馈到隐藏状态重建中。

Result: 在三个基准动力系统实验中，可见—可见结构推断持续提升，同时在隐藏状态重建与未来预测方面也显示收益；在模拟整肢遮挡的动作捕捉实验中，进一步验证了其在真实隐藏智能体场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SIHA能够有效缓解隐藏智能体轨迹与交互之间的循环依赖问题，实现隐藏轨迹与交互结构的联合恢复，并提升结构推断、状态重建与未来预测性能。

Abstract: Recovering latent interaction structures from multi-agent dynamics is important for understanding and predicting interacting systems. Trajectory-based structural inference has achieved promising performance, but conventional formulations assume that the trajectories of all modeled agents are available. In practice, agents may become unobserved at deployment because of limited sensing, occlusion, or communication failure. Existing studies have considered unseen-node estimation, structural inference under partial observations, and missing-value imputation, yet the joint recovery of hidden-agent trajectories and their interactions remains underexplored. We formulate this problem as structural inference under hidden agents. Its key difficulty is a circular dependency: recovering interactions involving a hidden agent requires an estimate of its trajectory, while trajectory reconstruction can itself benefit from structural information. To address this challenge, we propose Structural Inference under Hidden Agents (SIHA), which combines structure-agnostic initialization with structure-guided iterative refinement. SIHA reconstructs hidden trajectories from visible observations, infers interactions using Neural Relational Inference, and feeds the estimated structure back into hidden-state reconstruction through multi-strength structural attention and iterative state--structure updates. Experiments on three benchmark dynamical systems demonstrate consistent improvements in visible-to-visible structural inference, while also showing benefits in hidden-state reconstruction and future prediction. Motion-capture experiments with simulated whole-limb occlusion further demonstrate its effectiveness in realistic hidden-agent settings.

</details>


### [217] [Regional Explanations via Causal Sufficiency and Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18049)
*Xuexin Chen,Peng Liang,Zijian Li,Zhiyong Lin,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SNRE框架，学习输入区域A与输出区域B，使A的隶属对模型输出落入B既充分又必要，并基于PNS构造可微有限样本估计器进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法多通过特征重要性、反事实解释或规则来解释预测，但对"何时且仅当"某预测行为出现的区域级刻画研究较少。

Method: 受经典概率必要性与充分性(PNS)启发，通过随机干预定义区域级PNS度量，推导可微有限样本估计器；用显式可解释的代数区域族参数化输入-输出区域对，并结合可学习特征掩码。

Result: 实验表明SNRE学习到的区域对具有强充分性-必要性性能、稳健的解释行为以及对模型分析的实际效用。

Conclusion: SNRE实现了区域级的充分必要解释，兼顾表达力与可解释性，为模型分析提供实用工具。

Abstract: Model explainability is essential for understanding and trusting machine learning models. Existing explainable AI methods often explain predictions through feature importance, counterfactual explanations, or rules. However, a region-level characterization of when and only when a prediction behavior arises remains less explored. This paper proposes Causal Sufficient and Necessary Regional Explanations (SNRE), a framework that learns an input region $A$ and output region $B$ such that membership in $A$ is both sufficient and necessary for the model output to fall in $B$. Motivated by the classical Probability of Necessity and Sufficiency (PNS), we formulate a region-level PNS measure through stochastic interventions and derive a differentiable finite-sample estimator for optimization. SNRE parameterizes the input-output region pair with explicit and interpretable algebraic region families, together with a learnable feature mask, balancing expressiveness and interpretability. Experiments demonstrate that SNRE learns region pairs with strong sufficiency-necessity performance, robust explanation behavior, and practical utility for model analysis.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Embedding Transfer: Component Roles in Grokking Transfer and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18078)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在模算术跨算子迁移中，将转移增益分解为"加速效果"与"稳定性"，发现转移内部注意力/MLP权重可显著提升早期精度并降低确认延迟，同时揭示继续训练会引发严重的"后grokking"回退，而冻结转移的表征载体与在线门控能有效抑制回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热启动迁移能让算法任务快速泛化，但尚不清楚模型中哪些组件贡献了增益，以及该增益在持续优化下是否稳定。作者希望区分迁移的"效果（早期速度）"与"稳定性（达到目标后的回退）"。

Method: 在模算术上进行跨算子迁移实验：采用规模匹配的108次运行（96次2^3因子设计加12次规模控制）；转移注意力/MLP内部权重（B）以及token嵌入和读出层（E+U）；使用Holm校正与TOST等价性检验；开展2层模型的预注册复现；并测试在非阿贝尔S_5上的转移表现，比较冻结表征载体（E、U）与在线验证触发门控等防护策略。

Result: 转移B加E+U使早期精度提高5.46个百分点（Holm p=0.0039），确认延迟减少558步（Holm p=0.0088）；2层复现确认内部模块优势（12/12种子，+704.67积分单位，p=4.88×10^-4）；但省略供体嵌入会大幅下降4475.6单位，超出±250容差。继续训练常引发严重后grokking回退；冻结E、U使离线回退从19.40%降至0.07%（Holm p=0.005859）；在线门控将最大回撤从22.06%降至0.60%（p=0.000488），并在仿射、非线性二次及2层任务中扩展到10.94–23.47个百分点的回退削减。在S_5中，未屏蔽转移出现95.4%的瞬时峰值，但预注册屏蔽组未获确认收益（+0.15±1.14个百分点）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了转移加速与轨迹稳定性在组件层面的解离，并界定了参数屏蔽方法的经验边界。

Abstract: Warm-start transfer can make algorithmic tasks generalize rapidly, yet it is unclear which model components provide the gain and whether that gain remains stable under continued optimization. We study cross-operator transfer on modular arithmetic and separate efficacy (early velocity) from stability (post-reach drawdown). In a scale-matched 108-run battery across 12 seed blocks (96-run 2^3 factorial plus 12-run scale control), transferring internal attention/MLP weights (B) alongside token embeddings and readout (E+U) improves early accuracy by 5.46 pp (Holm p=0.0039) and cuts confirmation latency by 558 steps (Holm p=0.0088). While readout plus internal-block transfer satisfies the pre-specified +/-500-step latency equivalence criterion in 1-layer models (TOST p=0.0011, though Full is faster in 11/12 paired seeds), a prospective 2-layer replication confirms the internal-block advantage (12/12 seeds, +704.67 integral units, p=4.88x10^-4) while revealing an architectural boundary: omitting donor embeddings falls 4475.6 units below Full, outside the +/-250-unit margin. Continued target training frequently triggers severe post-grokking relapse. Freezing transferred representation carriers (E, U) nearly eliminates offline relapse (19.40% -> 0.07%, Holm p=0.005859). Online validation-triggered gating slashes True Max Drawdown from 22.06% to 0.60% on 2a+b (p=0.000488), with prospective confirmations extending protection across affine, nonlinear quadratic, and 2-layer targets (10.94-23.47 pp reductions), distinguishing continual stabilization from static early stopping. In non-abelian S_5, unshielded transfer surges transiently (95.4% peak), but a prospective shielding cohort yields no confirmed benefit (+0.15 +/- 1.14 pp). These results establish a component-level dissociation between transfer acceleration and trajectory stability, and expose the empirical boundaries of parameter shielding.

</details>


### [219] [FedPGT: Progressive Gradient Transmission for Vehicular Federated Learning over Time-Varying Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18089)
*Jintao Yan,Tan Chen,Yuxuan Sun,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPGT，一种面向时变信道的车载联邦学习渐进式梯度传输方案，通过收敛性分析和Lyapunov优化实现在线调度，在图像分类和轨迹预测任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载联邦学习（VFL）虽通过通信资源分配和梯度稀疏化降低开销，但车辆移动性导致信道条件和传输容量快速变化，使预先设定的资源分配与稀疏化决策失效，亟需面向瞬时信道条件的在线决策方案。

Method: 提出FedPGT渐进式梯度传输方案，车辆根据瞬时信道条件逐步传输高幅值梯度条目。建立刻画传输梯度条目影响的收敛界，揭示服从幂律衰减的收益递减规律。基于此构建在线决策的随机优化问题，针对累积耦合、不可分目标，引入每时隙代理传输变量解耦长期依赖，转化为可加的每时隙优化问题，进而采用Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty方法实现在线调度，并设计低复杂度资源分配算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类任务上准确率提升3.65%，在Argoverse轨迹预测任务上平均位移误差降低12.66%，均优于当前最先进基线。

Conclusion: FedPGT能够适应高度动态的车载环境，适用于多样化的学习任务，有效提升车载联邦学习的训练性能与通信效率。

Abstract: Vehicular federated learning (VFL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training for intelligent transportation systems, where communication resource allocation and gradient sparsification techniques have been explored to reduce communication overhead. However, vehicle mobility leads to rapidly varying channel conditions and transmission capacity, rendering predetermined resource allocation and sparsification decisions ineffective. In this paper, we propose FedPGT, a progressive gradient transmission scheme for VFL over time-varying channels, where vehicles progressively transmit high-magnitude gradient entries in response to instantaneous channel conditions. We establish a convergence bound that characterizes the impact of transmitted gradient entries and reveals diminishing-return behavior governed by a power-law decay. Motivated by this result, we formulate a stochastic optimization problem for online decision-making, where the main challenge lies in a cumulatively coupled, non-separable objective. To handle this challenge, we introduce per-slot surrogate transmission variables to decouple the long-term dependence across time slots and convert the original objective into an additive per-slot optimization problem, enabling a Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty approach for online scheduling. We further develop a low-complexity resource allocation algorithm for efficient online implementation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves a 3.65% accuracy improvement on the CIFAR-10 image classification task and a 12.66% reduction in average displacement error on the Argoverse trajectory prediction task compared with state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating its applicability to diverse learning tasks under highly dynamic vehicular environments.

</details>


### [220] [Agora: Git as Shared Memory for Collective AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18094)
*Yifan Zhang,Yunheng Zou,Shaokun Zhang,Jian Hu,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agora 是一个面向自主研究智能体的共享记忆系统：研究过程被记录为存储在 Git 中的只增有向无环图（DAG），每条声明都是一个可检出、可复现的提交。近 12 天的首次持续使用中，13 个无任务分配、无中央规划的语言模型工作者，在权重迁移问题上发布了 1,703 项贡献，将评估指标从 3.39 降至 1.899 bits per byte，缩小了与训练后 GPT-2 124M 差距的 62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究循环（如 AutoResearch）中，单个编码智能体可无人值守地改进训练设置，但多个此类会话各自从零开始，导致更多智能体意味着更多重复搜索而非更多发现，缺乏共享研究状态。

Method: 将研究记录为 Git 中只增的 DAG，每个结果、洞见、假设、验证与报告都是不可变提交，父边表示其构建基础；派生索引暴露前沿、被忽视分支和每条声明的验证状态；采用多样性感知的选择规则，防止社区坍缩到单一领先者。实验设置：141 个预训练供体模型，目标为冻结的 119.6M 参数 attention-SSM 混合模型，维度与任何供体都不匹配，需在无训练数据、无梯度更新的条件下初始化。

Result: 13 个语言模型工作者在近 12 天内发布 1,703 项贡献，评估指标从 3.39 降至 1.899 bits per byte，缩小与训练后 GPT-2 124M 差距的 62%。获胜方案将供体的下一词元统计压缩到目标模型的嵌入与输出头，再通过稀疏编辑注意力、前馈与状态空间块加入短程上下文信号；其 145 个提交的谱系跨越 15 个账户，并发布了 165 次独立复现，无一失败。

Conclusion: 论文描述了运行中一次人工干预将社区从单一文化中拉出，说明了该轨迹能证明与不能证明的内容，并指出需要受控对比实验才能确定共享研究状态是否提升单位算力的发现效率。

Abstract: Autonomous research loops such as AutoResearch show that one coding agent can improve a training setup unattended. Run several of them and each session starts from scratch, so more agents tend to mean more duplicated search rather than more discovery. Agora is a shared memory for such agents: research is recorded as an append-only directed acyclic graph (DAG) stored in Git, so that every claim is a commit anyone can check out and rerun. Each result, insight, hypothesis, verification, and report is an immutable commit whose parent edges say what it builds on; a derived index exposes the frontier, the neglected branches, and the verification status of each claim, and a diversity-aware selection rule keeps the community from collapsing onto one leader. We describe the system and report its first sustained use: a run of nearly 12 days in which 13 language-model workers, with no assigned tasks and no central planner, worked on a weight-transfer problem. Given 141 pretrained donor models and a frozen 119.6M-parameter attention-SSM hybrid whose dimensions match no donor, the workers had to initialize the target without training data or gradient updates. They published 1,703 contributions and drove the evaluator from 3.39 to 1.899 bits per byte, closing 62% of the gap to a trained GPT-2 124M. The winning recipe compresses donor next-token statistics into the target's embedding and output head, then adds a short-range context signal through sparse edits to attention, feed-forward, and state-space blocks. Its 145-commit ancestry spans 15 accounts, and 165 independent reproductions were posted, none of which failed. We describe the single mid-run human intervention that pulled the community out of a monoculture, what the trace does and does not establish, and the controlled comparison that would settle whether shared research state improves discovery per unit of compute.

</details>


### [221] [iMINDBench: iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18104)
*Geeling Chau,Saba Hashemi,Yonghyeon Gwon,Eshani Patel,Jan DeWitt,Christopher Wang,Andrii Zahorodnii,Sabera J Talukder,Danny Dongyeop Han,Chun Kee Chung,Maryam M Shanechi,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出iMINDBench多机构iEEG神经解码基准，含15项任务、3个自然观影数据集，统一预处理与固定划分，发现预训练模型普遍优于基线但强频谱基线仍具竞争力，跨被试/机构增数据收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iEEG解码向通用基础模型发展时，缺乏可靠衡量进展的手段：数据集多为任务或机构特定，难以验证跨任务与跨记录环境的泛化性；预处理选择对性能影响大，使模型改进与预处理带来的增益难以区分。因此需要标准化的多机构基准。

Method: 构建iMINDBench基准，在三个自然观影数据集上以共享的15项解码任务评估模型；定义标准化预处理轨道（tracks）与固定评估划分以支持一致的模型比较；并开展数据规模缩放研究，引入其他被试或其他机构最多25倍监督数据。

Result: 在各自预处理轨道内，所评估的预训练系统总体优于基线；但强频谱基线在各机构数据集上仍具竞争力。缩放研究中，引入最多25倍外部被试/机构数据相比会话内训练仅带来小幅或任务相关的收益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，iEEG模型需要超越强预处理基线，并更有效地利用跨被试、跨机构的数据。项目网站：https://imindbench.github.io/

Abstract: Intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) is widely used to record electrical activity directly from electrodes inside the human brain, making it an attractive modality for neural decoding. However, progress in iEEG decoding, especially toward general-purpose foundation models, remains difficult to measure reliably: datasets are task- or institution-specific, limiting evidence of generalization across tasks and recording environments, and preprocessing choices can strongly influence performance, making model improvements difficult to distinguish from preprocessing gains. Thus, we introduce iMINDBench, an iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark that evaluates models on a shared suite of fifteen decoding tasks across three naturalistic movie-watching datasets. The benchmark additionally defines standardized preprocessing tracks and fixed evaluation splits to support consistent model comparisons. Using iMINDBench, we find that the evaluated pretrained systems generally outperform baselines within their respective preprocessing tracks, while strong spectral baselines remain competitive across institutional datasets. In our scaling study, adding up to 25 times more supervised data from other subjects or institutions yields only small or task-dependent gains over within-session training. Together, these findings highlight the need for iEEG models that improve on strong preprocessing baselines and make more effective use of data across subjects and institutions. Project website: https://imindbench.github.io/

</details>


### [222] [FoundAna: A GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18107)
*Suprim Nakarmi,Chahana Dahal,Yue Zhao,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FoundAna，首个面向图异常检测的基础模型框架，结合 GNN 与 Transformer，实现跨图泛化检测。在九个基准数据集上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法遵循“一数据集一模型”范式，受任务异质性、标签稀缺和领域差异影响，难以跨场景迁移，缺乏通用性。

Method: FoundAna 将异常检测专用 GNN 组件与标准 Transformer 编码器结合，并引入四种互补位置编码以捕捉局部与全局结构信息；经位置编码增强的节点表示通过属性与邻接解码器重建，以重建误差作为异常分数。

Result: 在涵盖金融、社交和引用网络领域的九个基准数据集上，FoundAna 一致优于当前最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: FoundAna 作为首个图异常检测基础模型框架，验证了跨图通用异常检测的可行性，代码与补充材料已开源。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify graph structures (e.g., nodes, edges, or subgraphs) that deviate significantly from expected patterns, which supports critical applications in fraud detection, spam identification, network intrusion, etc. Despite the growing methods in the field, existing approaches follow a one-model-per-dataset paradigm, limiting their transferability across diverse real-world scenarios due to task heterogeneity, label scarcity, and domain variability. In this work, we introduce FoundAna, a GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection - the first foundation model framework designated for generalizable, cross-graph anomaly detection by combining GNNs and transformers. FoundAna integrates an anomaly detection-specific GNN component with a standard transformer encoder augmented by four complementary positional encodings, which enable the model to capture both local and global structural information. Specifically, the positional encoding enriched node representations are passed through attribute and adjacency decoders, and the reconstruction errors serve as the anomaly score. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets spanning financial, social, and citation network domains demonstrate that FoundAna consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code implementation and Supplementary materials are here: https://github.com/FoundAna331/FoundAna.

</details>


### [223] [Learning Fractional-Order Dynamics from a Single Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18127)
*Xiaole Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zehao Zhao,Stephen Tu,Guannan Qu,Yorie Nakahira,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间分数阶线性时不变系统的系统辨识问题，提出两阶段的 FO-GS 估计方法，并给出分数阶次与系统矩阵均为 O(t^{-1/2}) 的非渐近高概率误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中许多过程具有长程依赖性，当前状态依赖于缓慢衰减的历史轨迹而非仅依赖最近状态。这类非马尔可夫动态可用 Grünwald–Letnikov 差分算子刻画，但分数阶系统会使估计在整段历史上耦合，给统计分析和实际辨识带来更大挑战，因此需要针对单条轨迹的系统辨识方法。

Method: 提出分数阶普通最小二乘网格搜索（FO-GS），一种简单的两阶段估计器。它利用分数差分算子的对角结构，将辨识问题按行解耦。

Result: 在稳定性假设下，针对异质情形建立了估计分数阶次与系统矩阵的高概率非渐近误差界，两者误差均以 O(t^{-1/2}) 速率缩放。实验表明 FO-GS 在恢复分数阶次和底层系统动态方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FO-GS 为分数阶线性时不变系统的单轨迹辨识提供了一种简单有效的方法，并具备理论上的非渐近误差保证，实验中优于现有基线。

Abstract: Many real-world processes exhibit long-range dependence, where the current state depends on a slowly decaying trace of past states rather than on the most recent state alone. This paper studies system identification for discrete-time fractional-order linear time-invariant systems from a single observed trajectory of length $t$, a setting that captures such non-Markovian dynamics through the Grünwald--Letnikov difference operator. Unlike Markovian systems, fractional-order systems couple estimation across the entire history, making both statistical analysis and practical identification more challenging. We propose \emph{Fractional-Order Ordinary-Least-Squares Grid-Search (FO-GS)}, a simple two-stage estimator that exploits the diagonal structure of the fractional-difference operator to decouple the identification problem row-wise. Under the stability assumption, we establish high-probability, non-asymptotic error bounds for estimating both the fractional order and the system matrix in the heterogeneous setting, with both estimation errors scaling as \(\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})\). Through experiments, we show that \emph{FO-GS} outperforms existing baselines in recovering both the fractional order and the underlying system dynamics.

</details>


### [224] [Colla-Q: Toward Collaborative Experts in MoE Quantization via Minimax Precision Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18131)
*Eunju Shin,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Colla-Q，一种基于激活熵的混合专家（MoE）量化比特分配框架，通过平衡各专家性能来提升量化后MoE模型的整体表现并降低对校准数据集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化虽能降低内存和计算成本，但会显著损害性能。在MoE模型中尤为严重，因为单个专家参数量小，对低比特表示敏感。由于MoE作为集成模型依赖各路由专家的协作贡献，某个专家因量化导致性能大幅下降会拖累整体模型性能。

Method: 提出Colla-Q比特分配框架，采用基于激活熵的比特宽度分配算法，为不同专家分配不同比特宽度，以维持各专家之间的性能平衡，促使各专家在量化模型中协同工作。

Result: 该方法1)提升了整体MoE性能；2)降低了模型对校准数据集的依赖；3)由于统一调节各专家性能，增强了MoE模型的鲁棒性和稳定性，使其在不同校准数据集上泛化更一致。

Conclusion: 通过激活熵驱动的比特分配实现专家间性能均衡，Colla-Q在提升量化MoE性能的同时减少了对校准数据的依赖，并提升了跨不同校准数据集的泛化一致性。代码已开源。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) quantization method based on activation entropy. Although quantization reduces memory and computational costs, it can substantially degrade performance. In particular, performance decline is pronounced in quantized MoE models, where individual experts have a small number of parameters that are sensitive to low-bit representation. Considering that MoE operates as an ensemble model with collaborative contributions from routed experts, a significant performance decline of a particular expert due to quantization can harm model performance. Therefore, we propose Colla-Q, a bit-allocation framework to maintain balanced performance across experts through an activation-entropy-based bit-width allocation algorithm. This approach encourages each expert to operate collaboratively in the quantized model, thereby 1) improving the overall MoE performance and 2) reducing the dependence on the calibration dataset. Since uniformly adjusting each expert's performance facilitates robustness and stability of the MoE model, the proposed MoE quantization method can generalize more consistently across different calibration datasets. Our code is available at: https://github.com/mmai-laboratory/Colla_Q

</details>


### [225] [Rethinking How We Evaluate Methodological Progress in Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18134)
*Florent Pollet,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在统一框架内重实现12种算法并在MIMIC-IV与NWICU上评测，发现算法间的总体成对比较在不同任务与数据集间高度可迁移，但临床有意义任务存在更强的任务-方法交互，且新算法并未稳定优于旧方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在电子健康记录（EHR）领域的方法学进展取决于能否判断哪些算法更好、在何种条件下更好。然而，可复现性困难与临床有意义评估任务难以定义被认为阻碍了这类进展，作者希望实证研究这些障碍。

Method: 作者在共享评估框架内重实现12种历史与近期算法，在MIMIC-IV和NWICU两个临床数据集上评测；比较两类互补任务族：专家撰写的临床有意义任务，以及由随机采样事件编码和预测窗口生成的生成式任务；考察算法相对比较能否跨任务族与数据集迁移、残余任务异质性是否包含有用方法学结构，以及受控比较所揭示的十年进展。

Result: 聚合的成对比较在评估设置间强迁移，包括从随机生成任务迁移到临床有意义任务以及跨数据集。同时，临床有意义任务表现出更强的任务-方法交互，初步表明任务属性可帮助解释特定建模选择何时更有利。此外，新算法并未持续优于早期方法：当搭配现代、宽而稀疏的EHR表示时，梯度提升树仍极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有用的方法学知识可能并不像通常假设的那样需要大量任务工程，同时凸显理解任务与方法间结构性异质性的重要性。

Abstract: Methodological progress in artificial intelligence (AI) for electronic health records (EHRs) depends on our ability to determine which algorithms work better, and under which conditions. However, such progress is thought to be hindered by difficulties in reproducibility and in defining clinically meaningful evaluation tasks. We empirically study these barriers by re-implementing 12 historical and recent algorithms within a shared evaluation framework and evaluating them on two clinical datasets, MIMIC-IV and NWICU. We compare two complementary task families: expert-authored clinically meaningful tasks and generated tasks defined from randomly sampled event codes and prediction horizons. We ask whether relative algorithms comparisons transfer across task families and datasets, whether residual task heterogeneity contains useful methodological structure, and what a controlled comparison reveals about progress over the last decade. We find that aggregate pairwise comparisons transfer strongly across evaluation settings, including from randomly generated tasks to clinically meaningful tasks and across datasets. At the same time, clinically meaningful tasks exhibit greater task-method interaction, providing preliminary evidence that task properties can help explain when particular modeling choices are advantageous. Finally, newer algorithms do not consistently outperform earlier approaches: gradient-boosted trees remain highly competitive when paired with a modern, wide and sparse representation of the EHR. Together, these results suggest that useful methodological knowledge may require less task engineering than commonly assumed, while highlighting the importance of understanding the structured heterogeneity that remains across tasks and methods.

</details>


### [226] [Reaching Every Position Without Searching: Rotating Sparse Wiring on the Hypercube as a Substitute for Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18145)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用旋转稀疏连线替代注意力：每层仅2n条链接即可在log₂n层内实现全连接，性能优于全注意力，参数量和时间更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制在每个层、每个输入上都要付出搜索"连接谁"的代价（全连接为n²复杂度）。作者探究：固定、稀疏、逐层简单旋转的连线方式能走多远。

Method: 将序列n个位置视为log₂n维超立方体顶点，第ℓ层仅连接第ℓ mod log₂n维的邻居，使信息在log₂n层内可达所有位置，每层仅2n条链接。在合成任务、enwik8字符级语言建模及混合语料上测试，并对比混合方案（2层注意力+16层稀疏）与纯旋转调度。

Result: 合成任务上，旋转连线以1/32链接量匹配全连接；固定稀疏模式失败，关键是"触及每个维度"而非顺序。enwik8上，混合模型比同宽全注意力低0.06 bits-per-character，链接仅1/7，参数少42%，墙钟时间少2.4×；纯旋转与混合持平。第二语料差距扩大至0.16。可用学习率窗口宽4至8倍。

Conclusion: 固定旋转的稀疏连线可替代注意力实现高效信息传播，性能与效率兼优。同时报告了失败尝试（学习坐标、动力学变体）及测量规范（冻结语料、全覆盖评估、种子分布作排名标准）。

Abstract: Attention pays, at every layer and for every input, the cost of searching for whom to connect. We ask how far one can get with wiring that is fixed, sparse, and simply rotated from layer to layer. Treating the $n$ positions of a sequence as the vertices of a $\log_2 n$-dimensional hypercube and connecting each position, at layer $\ell$, to its neighbour along dimension $\ell \bmod \log_2 n$, information from every position reaches every other in $\log_2 n$ layers with $2n$ links per layer instead of $n^2$. On a synthetic task that is unsolvable unless all positions are reached, this rotation matches all-to-all wiring at $1/32$ of the links, while the same sparse pattern held fixed across layers fails; what matters is that every dimension is touched, not the order. On character-level language modelling of a public corpus (the first $12$M characters of enwik8), a hybrid that keeps two attention layers among sixteen sparse ones reaches $0.06$ bits-per-character lower held-out loss than a fully attentive model of the same width at the same step budget (three seeds each, no overlap), with $1/7$ of the links, $42\%$ fewer parameters, and $2.4\times$ less wall-clock time; the purely rotated schedule is level with the hybrid. The same ordering holds on a second corpus of mixed Japanese, English and code, where the gap widens to $0.16$. The usable learning-rate window is four to eight times wider than attention's on both. We also report what did not work - learned coordinates, and a "dynamics" variant whose apparent gains turned out to be an artefact of a saturated kernel - and the measurement discipline (frozen corpus, full-coverage evaluation, seed spread as the bar for ranking) that we found necessary to say anything at all at this scale.

</details>


### [227] [LIGE-GR: A Smooth Leap from Ranking to Generative Recommendation in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18148)
*Venkat Srinivas,Chenzhang He,Sam Woodmansee,Shawn Lian,Wenjie Hu,Renjie Jiang,Ziheng Huang,Xinyuan Zhang,Zhihao Zheng,Zhuoran Yu,Rui Li,Lei Yuan,Ziwei Li,Jimmy Jia,Mert Terzihan,Ekrem Kocaguneli,Yiming Liao,Zhichen Zhao,Yue Yin,Yue Weng,Wanlin Ma,Xufeng Cai,Weimiao Wu,Yezhou Huang,Du Zhang,Yukun Ding,Aaron Johnston,Yueming Wang,Zhaojie Gong,Yuting Zhang,Serena Li,Adithya Ganesh,Boying Liu,Haichuan Yang,Xialu Li,Matt Ma,Qunshu Zhang,John Joshua Miller,Praveen Rathinavelu,Cheng Huang,Aadhar Sachdeva,Josh Karns,Andres Aaron Gutierrez,Neil Agarwal,Gustas Pladis,Vladimir Batygin,Gopal Ray,Aditya Priyadarshi,Shantanu Patil,Zhe Wang,Penny Pan,Yiping Han,Arun Singh,Guangdeng Liao,Bi Xue,Xinyao Hu,Yang Song,Yisong Song,Meihong Wang,Haotian Wu,Deepak Agarwal,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LIGE-GR 框架，将传统逐项排序推荐升级为列表级生成式推荐，在保留现有系统兼容性的同时获得列表级优化收益，并在 Instagram Reels 与 Facebook Video 上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的成功为下一代推荐系统提供启示：推荐与语言生成在结构上都旨在生成优化用户体验的有序序列。然而，如何将 LLM 范式的精髓精确融入成熟的工业推荐系统仍是未解难题。具体存在两大挑战：一是如何把 LLM 范式中的序列级生成与优化引入推荐；二是真实工业推荐系统经过多年围绕产品、业务约束、服务基础设施和组织归属的迭代定制，整体替换风险高且组织阻力大。

Method: 提出 LIGE-GR，一种列表级生成与评估的推荐框架。它不重建整个推荐栈，而是将已有的逐项（pointwise）推荐系统泛化为列表级（listwise）生成系统，从而在保持与现有模型、价值函数和服务基础设施兼容的前提下，使成熟推荐系统获得列表级优化的收益。

Result: 在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 的短视频推荐场景中验证：Instagram Reels 的用户停留时长提升 1.14%，Facebook Video 提升 0.72%，且仅需适度增加的推理资源。

Conclusion: LIGE-GR 表明，无需推翻现有工业推荐系统，即可通过列表级生成与评估的渐进式升级，从 LLM 范式中获益，实现性能提升与工程可行性、组织兼容性的平衡。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has provided important inspiration for the next generation of recommender systems. Structurally, recommendation and language generation share a similarity: both aim to produce an ordered sequence that optimizes the user's experience. However, how to precisely absorb the essence of the LLM paradigm into mature industrial recommender systems remains an open problem.
  There are two challenges. First, it is unclear how to incorporate sequence-level generation and optimization from the LLM paradigm into recommendation. Second, real-world recommender systems are mature systems that have been iteratively customized for years around specific products, business constraints, serving infrastructure, and organizational ownership. Replacing such systems wholesale is often technically risky and organizationally disruptive.
  In this paper, we propose LIGE-GR, a listwise generation and evaluation recommendation framework that upgrades from a traditional ranking system based on itemwise recommendation toward a generative recommendation paradigm. Instead of rebuilding the entire recommendation stack from scratch, LIGE-GR generalizes the existing pointwise recommendation system into a listwise generation system. This allows mature recommender systems to benefit from listwise optimization while preserving compatibility with existing models, value functions, and serving infrastructure.
  We validate LIGE-GR in short-video recommendation on Instagram Reels and Facebook Video. On these recommendation surfaces, LIGE-GR improves time spent by 1.14 percent on Instagram Reels and 0.72 percent on Facebook Video, while requiring only modest additional inference resources.

</details>


### [228] [MoRE: Mixture of Reused Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18176)
*Eric S. Qiu,Utku Umur Acikalin,Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Arjun B. Mulchandani,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Mixture of Reused Experts (MoRE)架构，通过相邻层共享专家池并结合深度嵌入，在参数量和计算量匹配下取得更低困惑度和更强下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然将模型容量与计算成本解耦，但参数量随专家数量线性增长，导致内存占用高；而循环Transformer虽能通过复用层权重实现参数高效，却缺乏竞争性的语言建模能力。因此需要一种兼具参数效率与建模能力的方案。

Method: MoRE让相邻层组成的一组层共享同一个专家池，每层保留独立的路由器，从更大的共享池中选择专家，从而在不增加参数的情况下扩大路由组合多样性。为使共享专家能够区分不同层，引入轻量级可学习的深度嵌入，在路由前对每层输入进行条件化。该方法对现有MoE实现仅需极小改动。

Result: 在三种模型规模（114M–1.15B参数）上的实验表明，MoRE在计算量和参数量匹配的条件下，困惑度始终低于标准MoE和当前最先进的权重共享架构，下游任务表现也更强。

Conclusion: MoRE通过共享专家池与深度嵌入的结合，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升语言建模性能，是一种可无缝集成到现有MoE实现中的高效混合架构。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures decouple model capacity from computational cost, yet incur high memory footprints as parameters grow linearly with the number of experts. Recurrent Transformers achieve parameter efficiency by reusing layer weights, but typically lack the capacity for competitive language modeling. We propose Mixture of Reused Experts (MoRE), a hybrid that shares expert pools across groups of adjacent layers. Each layer retains its own router but selects from a larger shared pool, expanding the diversity of routing combinations without additional parameters. To enable shared experts to distinguish between layers, we introduce lightweight learnable depth embeddings that condition each layer's input before routing. Experiments across three model scales (114M-1.15B parameters) show that MoRE consistently achieves lower perplexity and stronger downstream performance than standard MoEs and state-of-the-art weight-sharing architectures at matched compute and parameter budgets, with only minimal modifications to existing MoE implementations.

</details>


### [229] [Transformation Laws in Neural Representations: Structure, Realisability, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18190)
*Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过变换律(transformation laws)这一具体对象，将神经表示的理解与设计相连接：理论上刻画变换何时能通过编码器下降及其缺陷结构，实证上以色相为例测量冻结视觉特征中的调和轨道组织，并据此构造出无需拟合的零样本色相读取接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表示如何保留输入变化的结构，这一问题是表示分析与内部干预之间的连接点。作者希望研究"可操作的表示内容"，即参考变换能否以相容动作作用于神经特征，从而把"理解表示"与"设计表示"统一起来。

Method: 理论上：刻画变换何时能沿编码器下降，给出线性设定下缺陷由变换对"被丢弃信息"的需求所支配（以表示诱导的度量衡量）；对整流器(rectifier)区分失效的两个来源；对实测调和载体给出闭式判据——当保留的调和块在作用下不变时存在线性实现。实证上以色相为深度实例，测量冻结视觉特征中色相轨道的能量分布、旋转平面结构及其来源（输入、架构、训练、深度）。构造上：依据测量结果搭建紧凑接口，其旋转作用由结构固定、从不拟合，实现零样本色相读取。

Result: 冻结视觉特征中色相轨道84–88%的能量集中在前两个调和分量，旋转平面在不同形状间共享；该组织大量继承自输入与架构，并被训练与深度重塑；测得结构支持预测、从新起始状态迁移和复合，全局与局部实现在这些能力上差异显著。所构造的接口在未见形状上以3.4°中位误差零样本读取色相。

Conclusion: 理论、结构测量与构造三方面共同确立：变换律是连接神经表示理解与表示设计的具体对象。

Abstract: How neural representations preserve the structure of input changes connects representation analysis with internal intervention. We study operable representational content through compatible actions of reference transformations on neural features. We characterise when a transformation descends through an encoder, and give a linear setting in which the defect is governed by the transformation's demand for discarded information, measured in the metric the representation induces. On a rectifier the failure to realise a transformation has two distinguishable sources --- what the source region has already made unrecoverable, and what it costs to satisfy every region the transformation visits with one operator --- and for a \textit{measured} harmonic carrier the same question has a closed answer: a linear realisation exists exactly when the retained harmonic blocks are invariant under the action. Using colour as the in-depth instance, we find that hue orbits in frozen visual features concentrate 84--88\% of their energy in the first two harmonics with rotation planes shared across shapes, that this organisation is substantially inherited from input and architecture and is reshaped by training and depth, and that the measured structure supports prediction, transport from new starting states, and composition --- with global and local realisations differing sharply in which they achieve. Guided by the measurements, we construct a compact interface whose rotation action is fixed by the structure and never fitted: it reads hue zero-shot at 3.4$^\circ$ median error on unseen shapes. Theory, structural measurement, and construction together establish transformation laws as a concrete object connecting the understanding of neural representations to their design.

</details>


### [230] [A Lightweight CNN Integrated Compact Convolutional Transformer for Multi-Scale Feature Learning and reducing computational complexity for breast cancer mammography image detection and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18212)
*Md Taimur Ahad,Ainuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种CNN与紧凑卷积Transformer（CCT）结合的框架用于乳腺癌钼靶分类，模型仅25万参数即在3个数据集上取得99%-100%准确率，并引入可解释AI提升临床信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在医学图像癌症检测中表现良好，但难以捕获长距离上下文依赖；同时ViT虽擅长长距离建模却参数庞大、资源消耗高。作者希望在资源受限的临床环境中构建一个既轻量又能捕获长距离依赖、且可解释的乳腺癌分类模型。

Method: 将CCT（紧凑卷积Transformer）架构接在CNN卷积层之后，利用CCT tokenizer把CNN提取的特征重塑为紧凑的patch token，并加入位置嵌入以保留空间结构；采用5折交叉验证在3组乳腺癌钼靶数据集上进行评估，并集成可解释AI（XAI）来解释分类过程。

Result: 模型参数量仅为250,435，在3个数据集上取得99%-100%的准确率，显示出良好的泛化能力；XAI提供了对乳腺癌分类过程的解释，增强了临床信任。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于计算机辅助诊断系统，尤其是资源受限的临床环境。其新颖性在于通过卷积tokenization与Transformer学习的结合，克服了CNN末层梯度退化问题；相比ViT更轻量，同时能有效捕获乳腺组织区域间的长距离依赖，在乳腺癌分类中表现出高效性。

Abstract: Over the years, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong capability in cancer detection and classification using medical images. However, CNN-based models often struggle to capture long-range contextual dependencies. In such scenarios, integrating Compact Convolutional Transformer (CCT) architectures after the CCT layer allows CNN-extracted features to reshape into compact patch tokens using a CCT tokenizer, followed by the addition of positional embeddings to preserve spatial structure. Using 5-fold cross-validation, the model was tested on 3 sets of breast cancer mammography. With only 250,435 parameters, the model achieved 99%-100% accuracy across 3 datasets, indicating robust generalization. Explainable AI (XAI) was integrated into the model to explain the breast cancer classification process to enhance clinical trust. The results indicate that the proposed framework is suitable for computer-aided diagnosis systems, particularly in resource-constrained clinical environments. The novelty of the proposed CNN-integrated CCT overcomes the limitation of CNN's gradient degradation in the last layers by integrating convolutional tokenization with transformer-based learning. Lighter than ViT, which is effective in capturing long-range dependencies, the model has also proven efficient in breast cancer classification by capturing long-range dependencies among breast tissue regions.

</details>


### [231] [APGEM: Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation for Quantum Reinforcement Learning on a Real-World CVRP Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18219)
*Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid,Mir Mohammad Yousuf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出APGEM，一种策略引导的自适应量子误差缓解控制器，在含噪NISQ硬件上在线选择ZNE、PEC、CDR与REM四种技术，用于量子强化学习求解CVRP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子强化学习（QRL）以变分量子电路表示策略，适用于CVRP等组合优化问题；但在NISQ硬件上退相干会降低保真度并破坏学习稳定性，而传统误差缓解是静态施加的，不感知学习上下文。

Method: APGEM基于保真度、熵与成本感知的效用函数，并在时序差分Q分数上采用ε-贪心规则，在线选择Zero-Noise Extrapolation (ZNE)、Probabilistic Error Cancellation (PEC)、Clifford Data Regression (CDR)与Readout Error Mitigation (REM)。在基于德里真实地标的城市物流CVRP测试床上评估，节点间使用测地距离成本，覆盖五种噪声类型与四个严重程度。

Result: 在该实例上，QRL智能体优于构造式启发式并接近元启发式；高噪声下缓解将近似比从0.84-0.87提升至0.92-0.94。控制器在短训练周期下以CDR为主，在长训练周期下均衡使用四种技术，表明存在真正的依赖机制的选择行为。

Conclusion: 这些初步结果说明，自适应、感知学习的误差缓解是通向抗噪QRL的一条实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum Reinforcement Learning (QRL) represents policies as variational quantum circuits (VQCs), making it attractive for combinatorial optimization such as the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP). On noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware, however, decoherence degrades fidelity and destabilizes learning, and conventional error mitigation is applied statically without regard to the learning context. We introduce Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM), a controller that selects among Zero-Noise Extrapolation (ZNE), Probabilistic Error Cancellation (PEC), Clifford Data Regression (CDR), and Readout Error Mitigation (REM) online, driven by a fidelity, entropy, and cost aware utility function and an epsilon-greedy rule over temporal-difference Q-scores. We evaluate on a realistic urban-logistics testbed, a Delhi-based CVRP over real landmarks with geodesic inter-node costs, exercised across five noise families and four severity levels. On this instance, the QRL agent outperforms constructive heuristics and approaches metaheuristics, while mitigation restores approximation ratios from 0.84-0.87 to 0.92-0.94 under high noise. The controller shifts from a CDR-dominated regime under short training horizons to a balanced deployment across all four techniques under longer horizons, indicating genuine regime-dependent selection. These preliminary results position adaptive, learning-aware mitigation as a practical route to noise-resilient QRL.

</details>


### [232] [Anomaly Detection in General Ledger Data: Results from a Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18228)
*Jan Gronewald,Alexander Michael Rombach,Sebastian Stephan,Peter Fettke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将日记账分录测试(JETs)与机器学习方法混合结合，以提升审计异常检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JETs是年度审计的强制环节，用于评估高风险审计领域和潜在重大错报。但基于领域知识设计的JETs只能检测已知模式，产生的候选清单往往非常庞大，需要审计师投入大量额外精力。为保证审计的经济效率，必须降低JET结果清单中的假阳性数量，而机器学习方法在此领域展现出改善异常检测的潜力。

Method: 本文为进行中的研究，探索了多种将JETs与机器学习方法以混合方式结合的途径，并提出了专门化的模型，以提升异常检测的检测性能和结果有效性。实验基于包含不同正常与异常日记账分录的合成数据开展。

Result: 研究提出了用于提高检测性能与结果有效性的专门化模型，实验基于不同类型的正常与异常日记账分录合成数据完成。作为进行中的研究论文，尚未给出最终的量化对比结果。

Conclusion: 将JETs与机器学习混合结合有望降低假阳性、提升异常检测的有效性，从而改善审计效率。

Abstract: Journal Entry Tests (JETs) are a mandatory part of annual audits to evaluate and assess both highrisk audit areas and potential material misstatements. However, as JETs are designed to detect known patterns based on domain knowledge, the resulting lists are often very large and require substantial additional effort from the auditor. To ensure the economic efficiency of the audit, the number of false positives in JET result lists must be reduced. Especially machine learning (ML) methods represent a promising approach to improve anomaly detection in this field. In this research in progress paper, we investigate different approaches on how to combine JETs with ML-methods in a hybrid manner. We present specialized models to increase the detection performance and validity of anomaly detection results to improve audit efficiency. The experiments are based on synthetic data consisting of different normal and anomalous journal entries.

</details>


### [233] [F-DACE: Fuzzy Disagreement-Aware Causal Evidence Fusion for Abstention-Safe Conversational Retail Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18238)
*Sourish Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-DACE决策层，用模糊隶属度融合多种因果估计，通过硬性否决机制在估计冲突时弃权，减少虚假推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察性决策支持系统常仅凭单一因果估计给出建议，当多个合理估计量互相矛盾时仍强行推荐，缺乏可靠的不确定性识别与弃权机制。

Method: 以因果机器学习为引擎：通过后门调整识别条件平均处理效应，用EconML DML因果森林与DoWhy线性回归估计，双向固定效应检验，并用约束优化转化为候选杠杆。F-DACE决策层将精度、倾向重叠、安慰剂反驳稳定性、区间重叠、方向一致性表示为模糊隶属度，并在估计目标不匹配、诊断失败、符号冲突或证据薄弱时硬性弃权。

Result: 在180个面板模拟、6种识别条件下，F-DACE在67.2%运行中做出决策，虚假推荐率为17.2%，优于因果森林33.3%与后门回归35.6%，与确定性一致法持平。31次虚假推荐中30次源于共同未测混杂。零售应用中，对5个降价指标全部弃权（部分估计不精确、一项反驳失败、MarkDown5符号冲突）。LangGraph对话代理在24个真实问题上达到100%工具路由准确率、100%状态保真度、0.983平均有据性，在10个对抗问题上全部抵抗注入指令。

Conclusion: 融合多估计并引入模糊隶属度与硬性否决，可有效降低虚假因果推荐；但共享未测混杂是任何融合规则都无法诊断的根本局限，故弃权而非强行推荐更稳健。

Abstract: Observational decision-support systems often expose one causal estimate as a recommendation even when plausible estimators disagree. The inherent engine of the proposed system is causal machine learning: a conditional-average-treatment-effect estimand identified by backdoor adjustment, estimated by an EconML DML causal forest and DoWhy linear regression, checked by two-way fixed effects, and converted into candidate levers by constrained optimisation. F-DACE is the decision layer on that engine. It represents precision, propensity overlap, placebo-refutation stability, interval overlap, and directional agreement as fuzzy memberships. Hard vetoes force abstention after estimand mismatch, failed diagnostics, informative sign conflict, or weak evidence. In 180 panel simulations spanning six identification conditions, F-DACE made a decision in 67.2% of runs and limited false recommendations to 17.2%; the corresponding rates were 33.3% for the causal forest and 35.6% for backdoor regression, matching deterministic unanimity rather than dominating it. Nearly all (30 of 31) false recommendations occurred under shared unmeasured confounding, which no fusion rule can diagnose when every component shares the omitted variable. The retail application aggregates a public Walmart panel to 6,435 store-weeks across 45 stores. F-DACE abstains for all five markdown indicators: some estimates are imprecise, one refutation fails, and MarkDown5 has a direct sign conflict. A LangGraph conversational agent exposes impact, what-if, and lever-optimization tools while a deterministic verifier preserves causal-layer status. On 24 live questions it achieved 100.0% tool-routing accuracy, 100.0% status fidelity, and 0.983 mean groundedness. On ten adversarial questions it resisted all injected instructions.

</details>


### [234] [Behavioral Fingerprinting and Navigation Prediction in Web Browsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18273)
*Ralph Elsaghbini,Omran Berjawi,Walid Fahs,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对会话级用户识别与下一域名预测两个行为推断任务进行对比实证研究，发现短浏览会话高度可识别、未来导航高度可预测，而LLM语义特征仅带来边际增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络浏览看似短暂，但短片段浏览活动也可能包含丰富且结构化的行为信号；作者希望系统检验浏览痕迹中用户可识别性与导航可预测性的程度，并比较不同建模思路（经典/神经模型、图模型、LLM）的贡献差异。

Method: 基于同一清洗后的事件流，在大规模匿名浏览痕迹上开展两个互补任务：会话级用户识别与下一域名预测。会话化与数据划分依据各任务的时间需求调整。用户识别使用传统与神经模型，基于会话级行为及域名特征；下一域名预测结合图建模与大语言模型。

Result: 短浏览会话具有高度可识别性；未来导航行为在长期交互结构结合近期行为上下文时高度可预测；LLM 导出的语义特征相比纯结构与序列模型仅带来边际提升。

Conclusion: 在评估的网络浏览场景中，重复交互模式仍是主导性的预测信号；交互历史对用户可识别性与导航可预测性均有显著贡献。

Abstract: Web browsing often appears ephemeral: users visit a few websites, complete a task, and move on. However, even short fragments of browsing activity can contain rich and structured behavioral signals. In this work, we conduct a comparative empirical study of two complementary behavioral inference tasks: session-level user identification and next-domain prediction. Both tasks are derived from the same cleaned event stream and evaluated on large-scale anonymous browsing traces, with sessionization and splitting adapted to the temporal requirements of each task. For user identification, we evaluate classical and neural models operating on session-level behavioral and domain features. For next-domain prediction, we combine graph-based modeling with Large Language Models (LLMs). Experimental results show that short browsing sessions are highly identifiable, while future navigation actions are highly predictable from long-term interaction structure combined with recent behavioral context. Furthermore, LLM-derived semantic features yield only marginal gains over purely structural and sequential models, indicating that repeated interaction patterns remain the dominant predictive signal in the evaluated web-browsing setup. These findings highlight the extent to which interaction history substantially contributes to both user identifiability and navigation predictability in browsing traces.

</details>


### [235] [A GAN-Based Framework for Robust DDoS Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18281)
*Makram Chehayeb,Walid Fahs,Amina Rizk,Rida Khatoun,Omran Berjawi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将WGAN-GP生成对抗建模与机器学习模型（随机森林、深度神经集成、Transformer）相结合的抗对抗攻击DDoS检测框架，通过在CICDDoS2019数据集上生成合成对抗流构建混合数据集训练模型，显著提升了检测精度与对未见对抗流量的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线服务的可用性与一致性易受DDoS攻击威胁，攻击策略日趋复杂，可绕过传统网络安全系统。机器学习模型虽能有效检测DDoS流量，但针对性的对抗攻击会降低其分类准确率，因此亟需提升检测模型的防御能力与鲁棒性。

Method: 框架整合生成对抗建模与先进机器学习模型：1）使用CICDDoS2019数据集训练随机森林、深度神经集成和Transformer模型，建立基线性能；2）采用带梯度惩罚的Wasserstein生成对抗网络（WGAN-GP）生成模拟潜在规避尝试的合成对抗流；3）将生成流量与良性、恶意流量组合构建混合数据集，训练模型学习更具泛化性的决策边界。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法显著提升了检测准确率与韧性，尤其针对未见过的对抗流量表现突出。同时，使用真实世界生成的流量对框架进行了测试，验证了其在实际场景中的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种可扩展且高效的抗对抗DDoS攻击解决方案，为构建更具韧性与自适应性的网络防御系统开辟了新路径，其核心在于将生成对抗增强与最新学习模型进展相结合。

Abstract: The availability and consistency of online services remain vulnerable due to Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks. These attacks are evolving by adopting more complex strategies to evade traditional network security systems. Despite the effectiveness of machine learning models in detecting DDoS traffic, targeted adversarial attacks can degrade their classification accuracy. This work proposes a robust detection framework that integrates generative adversarial modelling with advanced machine learning models. We trained Random Forests, Deep Neural Ensembles, and Transformer-based models using the CICDDoS2019 dataset to establish the frameworks baseline performance. To enhance the models defensive capacity, we generated synthetic adversarial flows that simulate potential evasion attempts and adversarial traffic using a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP). Then, we combined the generated traffic with benign and malicious traffic to construct hybrid datasets to train the models to learn more generalizable decision boundaries. The experimental results indicate that the proposed methodology significantly enhances detection accuracy and resilience, especially against unseen adversarial traffic. We also tested the designed framework using real-world generated traffic, which demonstrates its capability in practical settings. The scalable and efficient solution against adversarial DDoS attacks, introduced in this work, paves the way towards more resilient and adaptive network defense systems that combine generative adversarial augmentation with recent advances in learning models.

</details>


### [236] [Bias Amplification in Multi-Agent Network: How Biased Agents Shape Opinions and Rhetoric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18306)
*Omran Berjawi,Giuseppe Fenza,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨多智能体大语言模型系统中偏见意见如何通过文本交互传播。实验显示，少数持极端意见的智能体即可显著改变其他智能体的观点，且Llama 3.2模型中的观点转移比经典FJ模型更快。语义分析表明，修辞一致性与偏见暴露程度同步提升，且语言同化可能先于数值观点的明显转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益应用于智能体间交互的场景，其输出会影响集体推理与决策。尽管已有大量关于多智能体系统中LLM运作的研究，但偏见在此类系统中的传播过程仍是难题。本文旨在揭示偏见意见如何以文本交互形式在LLM环境中传播。

Method: 构建多智能体LLM环境：少数智能体保持持续极端意见，其余智能体通过结构化文本交互迭代更新信念。将结果与经典Friedkin-Johnsen（FJ）模型对比，并进行语义分析，考察修辞一致性与词汇采用情况。

Result: 即使只有小比例偏见智能体，也会导致非偏见智能体意见显著偏移；在相同偏见比例下，Llama 3.2模型的偏移速度快于经典FJ模型。语义分析显示，文本解释中的修辞一致性随偏见暴露系统性增强，且与数值收敛部分脱钩——中性智能体即便数值观点变化不大，也会采用偏见智能体的词汇。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多智能体语言模型生态系统中偏见与语言如何共同演化，表明语言层面的同化可能先于或独立于数值观点的转变，为理解偏见传播提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications involving interaction between agents, where their output plays a role in collective reasoning and decision-making processes. Despite significant research into the functioning of LLMs in such multi-agent systems, the processes of bias propagation in such systems are still a challenge. This work studies how biased opinions are propagated in the form of textual interaction in an environment of LLMs, in which a minority of agents maintain persistent extreme opinions, while the remaining agents iteratively update their beliefs through structured textual interactions. The findings show that even the presence of a small percentage of biased agents in such a system leads to significant shifts in the opinions of non-biased agents. It suggests that for the same percentage of biased agents, the shifts occur more quickly for the Llama~3.2 model when compared to a classical Friedkin-Johnsen (FJ) model. Further semantic analysis demonstrates that rhetorical consistency in textual explanations increases systematically with biased exposure and, importantly, is partially decoupled from numerical convergenumericalutral agents adopt the vocabulary employed by the biased agents even in configurations where their numerical opinion shifts remain moderate. The research helps explain how bias and language develop together in multi-agent language model ecosystems.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Quadratic Loss: The Stability Phase Diagram of Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18314)
*Gaoxiang Tang,Huanran Chen,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Adam 优化器中两个动量时间尺度如何共同导致损失尖峰，通过扫描 $(\beta_1,\beta_2)$ 平面发现一条近似线性的边界 $1-\beta_2=C(1-\beta_1)$ 区分尖峰与非尖峰训练动力学，并将该边界与损失的有限尺度超二次几何联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 损失尖峰是神经网络训练中反复出现的失稳现象，对 Adam 而言宏观尖峰与优化器动力学相关，但两个动量时间尺度（$\beta_1$、$\beta_2$）如何共同调控尖峰尚不清楚，因此需要系统性研究其依赖关系。

Method: 在 $(\beta_1,\beta_2)$ 平面上映射训练动力学，覆盖多种模型-任务设置；并借助一维二次损失 $L(x)\propto x^2$ 与一维超二次损失 $L(x)\propto|x|^n$ 进行理论分析，进一步分析 confident cross-entropy 损失的 core–wall 景观。

Result: 发现近似线性边界 $1-\beta_2=C(1-\beta_1)$ 区分尖峰与非尖峰动力学；一维二次损失给出近似三次方的斜率，而超二次损失 $|x|^n$ 可恢复近线性标度并把边界系数与有效损失指数 $n$ 联系起来；confident cross-entropy 损失形成由窄二次核心与陡峭墙组成的 core–wall 景观，在单次优化器更新尺度上呈现有效超二次行为。

Conclusion: Adam 损失尖峰源于动量时间尺度之间的不匹配，以及 Hessian 之外的有限尺度超二次损失几何，二者共同决定了失稳边界。

Abstract: Loss spikes are recurrent instabilities in neural-network training and can arise from multiple mechanisms. For Adam in particular, macroscopic loss spikes have been linked to optimizer dynamics, yet how its two momentum timescales govern them remains unclear. We investigate this dependence by mapping training dynamics across the $(β_1,β_2)$ plane. Across a range of model--task settings, an approximately linear boundary, $1-β_2=C(1-β_1)$, separates spiky from non-spiky dynamics, whereas a one-dimensional quadratic loss produces approximately cubic slope. A one-dimensional superquadratic loss $L(x)\propto|x|^n$ recovers the near-linear scaling and links the boundary coefficient to the effective loss exponent $n$. We further show that confident cross-entropy losses develop a core--wall landscape comprising a narrow quadratic core followed by a steep wall, which produces effective superquadratic behavior at the scale of an optimizer update. Together, these results connect Adam loss spikes to both the mismatch between momentum timescales and finite-scale superquadratic loss geometry beyond the Hessian.

</details>


### [238] [Multi-Appliance Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring via Label-Preserving Aggregate Recomposition and Prediction Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18315)
*Jiangfeng Liu,Yanfang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）的训练方法，通过"标签保持的聚合功率重组"构造配对窗口，并施加受可靠性准则与固定margin约束的预测一致性损失，在多设备MoE架构上实现跨家庭泛化提升，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NILM模型在源家庭训练后迁移到未见家庭时精度显著下降。聚合功率中含其他电器负载与测量误差，导致模型预测可能依赖与源家庭目标共现的残余背景，而非真正学习目标电器特征，从而损害泛化能力。

Method: 利用时间对齐的分表测量与聚合功率的可加分解，提出"标签保持的聚合重组"：仅替换某聚合窗口的残余背景，同时逐点保留所有已建模目标电器的功率序列，从而生成一个标签相同的配对窗口。对两个窗口均施加完整功率与运行状态监督；对每台电器，仅当两个预测都满足固定可靠性准则时，才惩罚其不一致，且只惩罚超过固定margin的部分。整体采用多电器架构，含两阶段"共享到专属"的混合专家（MoE）路由。

Result: 在REDD、UK-DALE与REFIT上，相对单窗口训练，电器平均MAE分别由14.75降至13.14 W、8.88降至8.51 W、15.83降至14.55 W。

Conclusion: 标签保持的聚合重组与预测一致性仅在训练阶段使用，不引入任何推理期模块或参数，因此在不增加部署成本的前提下提升了跨家庭泛化性能。

Abstract: Non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM) estimates appliance power sequences from aggregate power, but models trained on source households commonly lose accuracy in unseen households. Aggregate power also contains loads from other appliances and measurement error, so predictions may depend on the residual background that co-occurs with source-household targets. Time-aligned submetered measurements and the additive decomposition of aggregate power expose a relation unused by window-wise supervision: an aggregate window can be recomposed by replacing only its residual background while preserving all modeled target-appliance power sequences pointwise. We combine label-preserving aggregate recomposition with prediction consistency. Both windows receive complete power and operating-state supervision. For each appliance, disagreement between the two power predictions is penalized only when both satisfy a fixed reliability criterion and only to the extent that it exceeds a fixed margin. The proposed method is implemented using a multi-appliance architecture with two-stage shared-to-specific mixture-of-experts routing. On REDD, UK-DALE, and REFIT, the proposed method lowers appliance-averaged mean absolute error relative to single-window training from 14.75 to 13.14 W, from 8.88 to 8.51 W, and from 15.83 to 14.55 W. Label-preserving aggregate recomposition and prediction consistency are used only during training, and add no inference-time module or parameter.

</details>


### [239] [Trajectory Learnability for Offline On-Policy Distillation with Imperfect Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18321)
*Yihao Ai,Weilong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离线on-policy蒸馏中不完美教师监督的可学习性问题，提出一种基于“可学习性信号”的加权方法，提升蒸馏效果且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线on-policy蒸馏通过一次性收集学生轨迹和教师监督并重复使用来提升效率，但这也使得不完美的监督持续存在。即使强教师也会失败，而教师失败只是问题层面的粗略信号，并不意味着该学生轨迹上的所有监督都无用。因此需要探究：从不完美的教师监督中还能学到什么。

Method: 方法利用教师成功的问题定义一个廉价参考，代表学生能学到什么。先在教师成功的问题上训练，然后测量来自教师失败问题轨迹中每个已观测token的似然变化，用这种带符号的似然变化作为“可学习性信号”：增加越大表示该行为越被仅成功学习所强化。再将该信号聚合为轨迹级权重，用于原始蒸馏损失。该方法无需额外生成，可从存储的轨迹和模型checkpoint一次性计算。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，该方法相比离线OPD基线最多提升2.7个百分点，并在多个基准上达到或超过在线OPD变体。尽管增加了仅成功蒸馏阶段，它仅需2块GPU和约22 GPU小时，而代表性在线OPD方法需3块GPU和36–48 GPU小时。

Conclusion: 该方法通过廉价的可学习性信号对离线on-policy蒸馏损失进行轨迹级加权，在不增加生成开销的前提下有效利用不完美教师监督，同时兼顾性能与计算效率。

Abstract: Offline on-policy distillation gains efficiency by collecting student trajectories and teacher supervision once and reusing them throughout optimization. The same reuse makes imperfect supervision persistent. Since even strong teachers can fail, we ask \emph{what remains learnable from imperfect teacher supervision?} Teacher failure is only a coarse problem-level signal and does not imply that all supervision along the associated student trajectory is unhelpful. A natural alternative is to estimate teacher recoverability along the trajectory, but repeated continuations largely erase the efficiency advantage of offline distillation. We instead use teacher-successful problems to define a cheap reference for what the student can learn. We train on teacher-successful problems and measure how the likelihood of each observed token in trajectories from teacher-failed problems changes. We use these signed likelihood changes as an operational \emph{learnability signal}: larger increases indicate behavior more strongly promoted by successful-only learning. We aggregate this signal into trajectory-level weights for the original distillation loss. Unlike continuation-based estimates, our learnability requires no additional generation and can be computed once from stored trajectories and model checkpoints. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation, our method improves an offline OPD baseline by up to 2.7 percentage points and matches or outperforms online OPD variants on multiple benchmarks. Despite the additional successful-only distillation stage, it uses 2 GPUs and about 22 GPU hours, compared with 3 GPUs and 36--48 GPU hours for representative online OPD methods.

</details>


### [240] [Every Fixed Metric Has a Blind Spot: A Learned Atmospheric Critic for Scoring Forecast Realism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18381)
*Younes Elberkennou,Dmitri Demler,Thierry Meier,Luca Rispoli,Fanny Lehmann,Joel Oskarsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"学习式大气评论器"：训练判别器区分真实数据与模型输出，用其logit得到类散度的真实性分数，自适应检测天气模型的各种失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习天气预报模型在逐点指标上精度高，但存在模糊、周期性异常等非物理伪影。现有指标预先固定某种表示或变换，限制了其可检测的伪影类型。

Method: 训练一个判别器区分参考数据与模型输出场，取其输出logit作为类散度的真实性分数；判别器自动学习区分模型场与真实天气的特征，适应模型所表现出的任意失效模式。在ERA5再分析数据上施加多种合成损坏进行对比实验，并评估真实天气模型预报。

Result: 本方法成功识别各类损坏并对其严重程度排序，而现有指标至少在一种损坏上失效；对真实天气模型的评估显示，真实性分数随预报时效延长而下降，且该指标通常给数值模型高于机器学习模型的真实性评分。

Conclusion: 基于判别器学习的真实性度量无需预设表示或变换，能自适应检测未知失效模式，可作为评估天气模型真实性的通用指标。

Abstract: Despite their high accuracy on point-wise metrics, machine learning weather forecasting models can exhibit different failure modes such as blurring, periodic irregularities, and other unphysical spatial artifacts. This has motivated a variety of metrics to detect known failure cases. Existing metrics fix a representation or transformation in advance, and that choice limits the artifacts they can detect. We propose to train a discriminator for separating reference data from the model's output, and using its output logit to obtain a divergence-like realism score. The discriminator learns whatever separates the model's fields from real weather, adapting to whichever failure mode that model exhibits. We compare our learned atmospheric critic to existing metrics using various synthetic corruptions applied to ERA5 reanalysis data. Our method successfully identifies the corruptions and ranks their severity, while existing metrics fail on at least one corruption. Additionally, we evaluate forecasts from real weather models, and find that the realism score degrades with longer lead times and the metric generally assigns higher realism to numerical models than to machine learning models.

</details>


### [241] [Reliable Virtual Sensing: A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Robustness Under Sensor Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18396)
*Jens U. Brandt,Noah C. Puetz,Alexander Windmann,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MuViS-C，首个面向学习型虚拟传感在多域下抗常见传感器故障的鲁棒性基准，涵盖十种故障模式与多严重度，并在九数据集、六架构上评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟传感需从可用传感器测量估计难测物理量，但传感器失效时学习型预测器可能给出物理上不合理的估计，并传导为系统级故障，实际部署要求鲁棒性，而现有研究缺乏多域鲁棒性基准。

Method: 基于已有标称性能基准与腐败分类法，构建MuViS-C，覆盖十种传感器故障模式（从微弱漂移到灾难性信号丢失）及多严重度；提出互补鲁棒性度量：腐败下平均误差、相对性能退化、最坏情况脆弱性；在六个领域九个数据集上评测六种架构（梯度提升树及卷积、循环、注意力、MLP混合等序列建模归纳偏置），并对注意力架构探究三种鲁棒化策略。

Result: (i) 所有模型在腐败下显著退化，至少在一种腐败设置下劣于朴素预测器；(ii) 梯度提升树集成具有强鲁棒性；(iii) 专用鲁棒化可缩小注意力架构与最鲁棒模型的差距，但各策略都会损害标称性能。多域设计十分必要，模型排名随数据集变化，单一领域无法反映完整鲁棒性图景。

Conclusion: MuViS-C为学习型虚拟传感的传感器故障鲁棒性提供多域基准，揭示现有模型普遍脆弱、树集成较稳健、鲁棒化存在标称性能代价，且多域评测不可替代。基准开源，可扩展至新数据集、故障模式、度量与模型。

Abstract: Virtual sensing, the estimation of hard-to-measure quantities from available sensor measurements, is a critical enabler for control and monitoring in cyber-physical systems. However, when sensors fail, learning-based predictors can produce physically implausible estimates that propagate to system-level failures. We argue that real-world deployment demands robustness and introduce MuViS-C, the first multi-domain benchmark of robustness against common sensor failures in learning-based virtual sensing. Building on an existing nominal-performance benchmark and established corruption taxonomies, it covers ten sensor failure modes, from subtle drifts to catastrophic signal dropouts, at multiple severities. These are paired with complementary robustness measures capturing average error under corruption, relative degradation, and worst-case fragility. Across nine datasets from six domains, we benchmark six architectures spanning gradient-boosted trees and the major inductive biases for sequence modeling: convolution, recurrence, attention, and MLP-mixing. On the attention-based architecture, we further probe three robustification strategies. We find that (i) every model degrades substantially under corruption, becoming worse than a naïve predictor on at least one corruption setting, (ii) gradient-boosted tree ensembles achieve strong robustness, and (iii) dedicated robustification closes the gap between the attention-based architecture and the most robust models, though each strategy hurts nominal performance. The benchmark's multi-domain design proves essential, as model rankings shift across datasets, and no single domain captures the full robustness picture. MuViS-C is open-source and extensible to new datasets, failure modes, measures, and models.

</details>


### [242] [TERN: A Delta-rule Memory with a Seasonal Reference and Online Adaptation for Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18407)
*Shunya Nagashima,Yuta Funayama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TERN，基于带通道衰减与门控擦除的 delta-rule 快速权重记忆，结合季节参考与在线自适应，用于流感周度计数预测，在三个 Cola-GNN 基准上优于疫情图模型和通用预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周度流感监测计数对疫苗分配和公共卫生预警很重要，但难以预测：每个地区只有少数季节数据，波峰的时间和高度逐年变化；疫情增长期有用的信息在峰值后会误导，而上一季的形态则在一年内持续有效。现有疫情图模型和通用预测器只读取短固定窗口，对所有历史信息一视同仁，既无法利用更早季节，也不会在疫情阶段变化时丢弃过时关联。

Method: TERN 预测器，核心是 delta-rule 快速权重记忆，该记忆按通道衰减，并在由局部疫情阶段特征驱动的门控下沿学习到的地址进行擦除；同时结合显式季节参考和在线自适应。

Result: 在三个 Cola-GNN 流感基准上，TERN 优于疫情图模型和通用预测器，达到或超过季节参考模型；受控实验证实了记忆机制本身的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过具备阶段感知的快速权重记忆与季节参考、在线自适应的结合，TERN 能更有效地利用跨季节信息并适应疫情阶段变化，从而提升流感监测计数预测性能。

Abstract: Weekly influenza surveillance counts guide vaccine distribution and public-health alerts, yet they are hard to forecast. Each region offers only a few seasons, waves shift in timing and height every year, and information that helps while a wave grows misleads after its peak, whereas last season's shape stays informative for a year. Existing epidemic graph models and general forecasters read a short fixed window and treat all past information alike, so they neither exploit earlier seasons nor discard stale associations when the epidemic phase changes. To address these limitations, we propose TERN, a forecaster built around a delta-rule fast-weight memory that decays channel-wise and erases along a learned address under gates driven by local epidemic-phase features, combined with an explicit seasonal reference and online adaptation. On three Cola-GNN influenza benchmarks, TERN outperformed epidemic graph models and general forecasters, matched or exceeded seasonal references, and a controlled comparison confirmed the contribution of the memory itself.

</details>


### [243] [Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18466)
*Frantzeska Lavda,Maciej Falkiewicz,Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SANI（空间自适应噪声注入）采样框架，通过逐像素动态调整扩散采样中的噪声注入，改进图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样（DDPM/DDIM）在采样时对所有空间位置统一注入相同噪声方差，忽略了自然图像的空间几何特性：边缘、纹理等高曲率区域去噪器不确定、需要随机校正，而平滑区域分数精确，注入噪声反而有害。

Method: SANI引入概率门控机制，并结合推导出的空间自适应方差，在每个时间步以逐像素方式决定是否注入噪声及注入量，从而在复杂特征区域精细校正，同时保持结构良好的区域不被破坏。

Result: 实验及解耦消融表明，SANI在多种采样时间步下相较于vanilla DDPM和DDIM端点采样器持续提升FID，并与方差学习基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 空间自适应性在扩散采样中具有重要价值，逐像素的噪声注入能有效提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion samplers reverse a learned noising process using either stochastic (DDPM) or deterministic (DDIM) updates, which represent endpoints of a single family controlled by a scalar noise-injection variance that is applied identically at every spatial location. This uniform approach neglects the geometry of natural images: high-curvature regions such as edges and textures, where the denoiser is uncertain, benefit from stochastic correction, whereas smooth regions, where the score is precise, are degraded by injected noise. This work investigates whether each pixel requires stochastic correction at a given timestep and introduces Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection (SANI), a novel sampling framework that dynamically adjusts noise application on a per-pixel basis. SANI integrates a probabilistic gating mechanism with a derived spatially adaptive variance, ensuring that noise is injected precisely where needed to refine complex features while preserving well-formed structures. Experimental results and decoupling ablations demonstrate that SANI consistently improves Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) over the vanilla DDPM and DDIM endpoint samplers across diverse sampling timesteps, while remaining competitive with variance-learning baselines, highlighting the importance of spatial adaptivity in diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [244] [Provable Guarantees for Spectral Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18527)
*Violet Zheng,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带边翻转噪声的符号图上的二值节点标签恢复问题，提出一种基于噪声符号邻接矩阵主特征向量符号的简单谱方法，并给出与图结构无关的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化预测（如多标签联合预测）在自然语言处理和计算机视觉中广泛应用。作者希望为符号图上的节点标签恢复建立谱方法的理论保证，此前缺乏这方面结果。

Method: 针对Globerson等人（2015）引入的边翻转噪声符号图模型，采用简单谱方法：对噪声符号邻接矩阵求主特征向量，根据其特征向量符号解码节点标签。结合矩阵集中理论与特征向量扰动分析，推导新的集中不等式，显式量化邻接矩阵谱隙、节点数、度分布和噪声水平的影响。

Result: 获得节点标签近似推断的图结构无关理论保证，以及相对于真实标签的最大角度偏差保证；将一般结果与Cheeger常数关联，并给出不同图类的结果；通过多个合成实验验证理论。

Conclusion: 作者首次为基于谱的符号图节点标签恢复方法提供理论保证，并附带产生可能对其它机器学习问题有用的技术性结果。

Abstract: Structured prediction is the simultaneous prediction of multiple labels, and is widely used in various fields, such as natural language processing and computer vision. In this paper, we study binary node label recovery on signed graphs with edge-flip noise, a model introduced by (Globerson et al., 2015), via a simple spectral method that decodes node labels from the signs of the principal eigenvector of the noisy signed adjacency matrix. We develop graph structure-agnostic theoretical guarantees for approximate inference of node labels as well as guarantees for maximum angle deviation with respect to the ground truth node labels. By leveraging tools from matrix concentration theory and eigenvector perturbation analysis, we derive new concentration inequalities that explicitly quantify the effect of the spectral gap of the adjacency matrix, number of nodes, degree distribution, and noise level. As a corollary, we relate our general results to the Cheeger constant and provide results for different classes of graphs. We perform several synthetic experiments to validate our theory. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to provide theoretical guarantees for the spectral-based approach. As a byproduct of our analysis, we derive technical results that might be of independent interest and useful for other machine learning problems.

</details>


### [245] [Provable Guarantees and Efficient Learning of Structural Equation Models with Latent Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18535)
*Weijian Yu,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究含潜在混杂因子的线性结构方程模型，通过将观测变量的精度矩阵分解为稀疏矩阵与低秩矩阵之和，提出迭代识别末端节点并重建有向无环图的因果发现算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现旨在从观测数据中恢复因果关系，但在许多领域，潜在混杂因子的存在会使任务变得困难：忽略混杂因子会导致虚假关联和错误的边方向。因此需要一种能在潜在混杂因子存在下正确恢复因果结构的方法。

Method: 针对含潜在混杂因子的线性结构方程模型，提出一种迭代算法：逐步识别末端（观测）节点并重建观测变量的有向无环图。核心是将观测变量的精度矩阵恢复为"稀疏+低秩"结构——稀疏部分刻画观测变量间的条件依赖，低秩部分刻画少量潜在混杂因子的综合影响。

Result: 理论证明：对于 p 个观测变量、r 个潜在混杂因子和 s 条边，当样本量 n ≳ max{s log p, r p} 时，所提方法能正确识别观测变量间的有向因果关系。实验结果验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 该工作为存在潜在混杂因子情形下的线性因果发现提供了具有样本复杂度保证的方法，精度矩阵的稀疏+低秩分解是处理混杂因子的有效途径，理论与实验均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover causal relationships from observed data. In various fields, exploring causal relationships among variables remains an important topic, but this task becomes challenging due to the existence of latent confounders. Ignoring such confounders can lead to false associations and incorrect edge directions. In this paper, we study the linear structural equation model with latent confounders. We propose an algorithm that iteratively identifies terminal (observed) nodes and reconstructs the directed acyclic graph of the observed variables. To do this, we recover the precision matrix of the observed variables as a sparse plus low-rank matrix: a sparse matrix captures the conditional dependencies among observed variables, while a low-rank matrix captures the combined influence of a few latent confounders. We establish that for $p$ observed variables, $r$ latent confounders and $s$ edges, our procedure correctly identifies the directed causal relationship among observed variables, for $n \gtrsim \max\{s\log p,\ r p\}$ samples. Experimental results validate our theoretical contributions.

</details>


### [246] [Revisiting the Objective of Echo Chamber Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18545)
*Abylaikhan Bexeit,Kushani Perera,Shanika Karunasekera,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化社交网络"回音室检测"问题的目标函数，提出可扩展半定松弛算法，在合成与真实数据集上优于竞争方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回音室检测旨在找出对某话题意见一致、且与其余节点意见相左的节点集合，作者认为该问题既不同于社区发现等社交网络分析问题，也不同于最大割、最大团等图问题，且此前缺乏对回音室检测目标函数的正式形式化。

Method: 利用集合函数傅里叶变换理论（Stobbe and Krause, 2012）首次形式化回音室检测的目标函数，并提出可扩展的半定松弛方法，通过内点法与稀疏线性代数进行求解。

Result: 在小型合成实验中，算法比竞争方法更好地恢复真实回音室；在大型真实数据集上，算法得到的回音室具有更优的网络属性；在小型真实数据集上，算法找到的回音室与已被封禁用户的一致性高于竞争方法，从而独立验证了目标函数的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为回音室检测建立形式化目标函数并给出可扩展求解算法，实验表明其在合成与真实场景下均优于现有方法，并通过与封禁用户的一致性分析对目标函数进行了独立验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the detection of an echo chamber in a social network, i.e., the identification of a set of nodes that agree on a topic, while disagreeing with the rest of nodes. We argue that this problem is different from other social network analysis problems such as community detection, and from other graph problems such as maximum graph cut and maximum clique.
